TL;DR: 本文报告了一项关于AI密集型软件开发真实成本的案例研究：一个六人学生团队在一个学期内使用广泛AI辅助构建了完整的对话式入门助手。研究采用三层成本模型（真实AI支出、自我报告人力投入、人工反事实），初始报告19.4倍成本比率，但后续审查发现两个独立错误（在包月订阅下错误推断逐token成本、使用错误的区域劳动力价格估算反事实成本），修正后约为9.9倍。这两个错误具有普遍性，是该研究本身的重要发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于AI密集型软件开发真实成本的实证报告稀缺，且现有报告容易在最终数字中隐藏系统性错误，因此需要更严谨的成本测算方法。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，对六人学生团队为期一学期的AI辅助开发项目进行跟踪，构建三层成本模型（真实AI支出、自我报告人力投入、人工反事实），并通过后续审查发现和修正成本估算中的系统性误差。

Result: 初始成本比率为19.4倍，修正两个独立错误后为约9.9倍，原比率被高估约2倍。两个错误分别是：在包月订阅下错误推断每token成本、使用错误的区域劳动力价格估算反事实人力成本。

Conclusion: AI软件开发成本报告中的错误容易产生且难以察觉，需要更稳健、可复现的成本测算方法论来准确评估AI密集型开发的实际成本。

Abstract: Empirical reports on the true cost of AI-intensive software development remain scarce, and the few that exist are easy to get wrong in ways that never surface in the final number. We report early results from an ongoing case study: a six-person student team built a full conversational onboarding assistant -- RAG-based code chat, guided tours, dependency graphs, technical-debt analysis -- over one academic term using pervasive AI assistance. We instrumented development with a three-layer cost model (real AI spend, self-reported human effort, human counterfactual) and initially reported a 19.4x cost ratio. A follow-up pass revealed two independent errors -- inferring per-token cost under a flat-rate subscription, and pricing the counterfactual with the wrong regional labor rates -- that together had inflated the ratio by roughly 2x; the corrected figure is ~9.9x. We present this correction as an early, generalizable finding in its own right: both errors are easy to make, invisible in the final number, and plausibly common in similar reports. We outline next steps toward a more robust, replicable costing methodology for AI-intensive development.

</details>


### [104] [Does ISO-Grounded NFR Specification Improve LLM Code Generation? A Comparison of Rich and Structured Interventions against a Natural-Language Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13742)
*Joào Pedro Monteiro Pereira,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探究在LLM代码生成中，将非功能需求（NFR）用ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行语义增强（富自然语言或结构化JSON）是否能改善代码质量。结果显示ISO增强能提升静态质量代理指标（如不可读密度下降）并降低对提示词表述的敏感度，但不能可靠改善功能正确性，且NL-rich与结构化格式之间差异可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码生成中的非功能需求通常以简短的单行短语形式给出，缺乏丰富语义约束。研究者希望验证是否通过ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行语义增强（无论是自然语言还是JSON形式），可以比简单基线更好地生成代码。

Method: 以RobuNFR风格的单行基线（NL-simple）作为对照，在HumanEval/HumanEval-ET上评估四种NFR（性能、错误处理、代码异味、可读性），每个条件使用十种提示变体，固定模型快照，并采用配对非参数统计分析比较ISO-grounded的NL-rich（富自然语言）与Structured（结构化JSON）两种增强方式。

Result: 主要发现：ISO-grounded增强提升了静态质量代理指标，例如不可读密度在所有四种NFR上均下降（性能从0.88降至0.69），并降低了对提示词表述的敏感性；但未可靠改善功能正确性，错误处理场景下扩展测试通过率反而下降（防御性编码与精确输出基准之间存在张力）。次要发现：在ISO内容不变的情况下，NL-rich与Structured在正确性上差异可忽略（|delta| <= 0.023），语义内容比JSON或散文格式更重要。

Conclusion: 实践者应投资于基于标准基础的NFR内容编写，而非关注序列化形式的选择。研究提供了完整的可追溯复现包供后续研究使用。

Abstract: In LLM-based code generation, Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) are often specified as terse one-line phrases. We ask whether grounding those specifications in ISO/IEC 25010 Quality Model, either as rich natural-language prose (NL-rich) or as structured JSON (Structured), improves code generated on HumanEval/HumanEval-ET compared to a RobuNFR-style one-line baseline (NL-simple). We evaluate four NFRs (performance, error handling, code smell, readability) with ten prompt variations per condition under a fixed model snapshot and paired non-parametric analysis. Primary finding: ISO-grounded enrichment improves static quality proxies (unreadability density falls across all four NFRs (e.g., Performance 0.88 -> 0.69 for NL-rich)) and reduces sensitivity to prompt wording, but does not reliably improve functional correctness; for error handling, extended-test pass rate decreases, suggesting tension between defensive coding patterns and exact-output benchmarks. Secondary finding: when ISO content is held constant, NL-rich and Structured differ negligibly in correctness (|delta| <= 0.023), indicating that semantic content matters more than JSON-vs-prose format. Practitioners should invest in standard-grounded NFR content rather than serialization form. A fully traceable replication package is provided.

</details>


### [105] [Engineering Reliable Coding Agents: Evaluating and Operating the System Around the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13867)
*Stephanie Jarmak*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文档系统性地审视了AI编码代理从模型到系统部署的可靠性边界，提出将评估与操作视为依赖链的方法论，并构建了包含206条可靠性记录的版本化目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理通常以模型形式评估但以系统形式部署，其可靠性不仅取决于模型能力，还受限于执行环境、检索、内存、权限、审查界面和资源分配等系统层面因素。许多表面上的模型失败实际上源于系统其他环节。

Method: 采用结构化多声部综述方法，综合164篇学术文献、100条实践者记录、29条基准记录和17条作者系统案例记录；结合定向更新审计、软件工程覆盖分析和分布式系统证据综合进行多方验证。

Result: 贡献了版本化目录含206条可靠性记录（193条门控实践，其中56条深度开发，另有13条研究线索）；构建了证据分类账和代理生命周期依赖与修复不对称框架；提供可运行的评估与可靠性协议及5个可复用代理技能。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了系统级方法论，用于区分模型能力与基础设施效应、设计可辩护的评估方案，并构建组件失败时能够安全恢复的系统；同时明确指出综述是结构化的而非穷尽性的，证据强度随主题变化。

Abstract: AI coding agents are commonly evaluated as models but deployed as systems. Their reliability depends not only on model capability, but on the harness, execution state, retrieval, memory and state management, permissions, review interfaces, and resource allocation. This monograph examines those boundaries and develops a framework for evaluating and operating coding agents reliably. It synthesizes 164 scholarly works, 100 practitioner records, 29 benchmark records, and 17 author-system case records through a structured multivocal review, targeted update audits, software-engineering coverage analysis, and distributed-systems evidence synthesis. Across this evidence, many apparent model failures originate elsewhere in the system, while improvements at one layer often fail to propagate to end-to-end outcomes. Evaluation and operation are treated as a dependency chain in which weaknesses in task construction, execution environments, retrieval, state management, verification, or observability can invalidate downstream conclusions. The monograph contributes a versioned catalog of 206 reliability records: 193 gated practices, including 56 developed in depth, plus 13 research leads; an evidence ledger; a framework for dependency and repair asymmetry across the agent lifecycle; measurements and failure cases from operated agent systems; runnable evaluation and reliability protocols; and five reusable agent skills with evidence maps. Together, these provide a system-level methodology for distinguishing model capability from infrastructure effects, designing defensible evaluations, and building systems that recover safely when components fail. The review is structured rather than exhaustive, evidence strength varies by topic, and results depend on workload and configuration. The methods record which search lanes were executed, which remain unexecuted, and limits on evidence-grading claims.

</details>


### [106] [Engineering Signals of Human-AI Collaboration in the Agentic Coding Era: A Longitudinal Analysis of 33,228 Pull Requests from vLLM and SGLang with Implications for Biomedical AI Agents and Bioinformatics Pipeline Developmen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13884)
*Jiada Li,Xuesong Ye,Olamide Olowoniyi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对vLLM和SGLang两个高速AI基础设施开源仓库进行了纵向描述性分析，考察了七个工程指标（PR吞吐量、周期时间、贡献者多样性、PR评论密度、合并率、新作者参与度和PR大小）。结果显示AI辅助开发与开发速度大幅提升（吞吐量增长17.9-21倍）和AI-开发者协作信号增强相关，但增长主要来自人类驱动而非机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手和自主智能体开发系统的快速普及与开源软件工程节奏和结构的大幅变化同时发生，但团队层面的纵向实证证据仍然有限。

Method: 对vLLM（2023年2月-2026年6月，18,290个PR）和SGLang（2024年1月-2026年6月，14,938个PR）两个仓库的所有合并PR计算七个工程指标，将开发分为与AI辅助开发重大变化对应的四个阶段，并区分人类和机器人作者的活动。

Result: 两个项目均显示开发速度和AI-开发者协作信号大幅增加：vLLM PR吞吐量增长21倍，SGLang增长17.9倍，但机器人作者PR仅占增长的不到0.2%。最新阶段中位周期时间为vLLM 1.04天、SGLang 0.62天。月度唯一作者稳定增加。PR评论密度分别增长4.2倍和3.8倍，机器人评论贡献估计15-20%。PR大小在各阶段保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发与高速开源软件开发中更高的吞吐量、更广泛的贡献者参与和更强的AI-开发者协作信号相关联，且增长主要由人类驱动。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding assistants and autonomous agentic development systems has coincided with major changes in the pace and structure of open-source software engineering. Yet empirical longitudinal evidence of these changes at the team level remains limited. We present a descriptive longitudinal analysis of seven engineering metrics: pull request (PR) throughput, cycle time, contributor diversity, PR comment density, merge rate, new-author participation, and PR size. Metrics were computed from all merged PRs in two high-velocity AI infrastructure repositories, vLLM (February 2023-June 2026; 18,290 PRs) and SGLang (January 2024-June 2026; 14,938 PRs). We segment development into four eras aligned with major changes in AI-assisted software development and examine human- and bot-authored activities. Both projects show substantial increases in development velocity and AI-developer collaboration signals. PR throughput increased 21x in vLLM and 17.9x in SGLang, while bot-authored PRs accounted for less than 0.2% of this growth, indicating that the increase was overwhelmingly human-driven. In the latest era, median cycle time was 1.04 days for vLLM and 0.62 days for SGLang, while P90 cycle times reached 16.8 and 14.3 days, respectively. Monthly unique authors increased steadily in both projects, suggesting broader contributor participation. PR comment density increased 4.2x in vLLM and 3.8x in SGLang, with bot comments contributing an estimated 15-20% of the increase. In contrast, PR size remained relatively stable across eras. Overall, AI-assisted development is associated with higher throughput, broader contributor participation, and increased AI-developer collaboration signals in high-velocity open-source software development.

</details>


### [107] [Rethinking Automated Program Repair: The Impact of Bug Complexity, Fault Localization, and LLM Cost-efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14065)
*Junchi Liu,Ali Bigdeli,Roya Daneshi,Atu Ambala,Sudipto Ghosh,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动程序修复（APR）技术进行了全面的实证分析，评估了两种APR技术配合三种LLM在不同缺陷复杂度、故障定位策略和推理设置下的修复性能与成本效益，发现即使面对复杂缺陷和不精确的定位，LLM-based APR仍具竞争力，但高成本LLM和强推理设置并不总能带来更好的成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM-based APR的整体修复效果，而缺陷复杂度、故障定位、推理设置及修复成本效益等因素的影响尚未得到充分探索，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用多维实证框架和统计分析，评估两种APR技术（ChatRepair和CodeCorrector），结合三种LLM（DeepSeek、GPT和Llama），在不同缺陷复杂度和定位策略下进行系统性的性能比较。

Result: 结构复杂缺陷和不精确故障定位会增加修复难度，但LLM-based APR仍能达到有竞争力的修复效果；不精确的故障定位会显著扩大不同APR技术之间的性能差距；更高成本的LLM和更强的推理设置并不一致地带来更好的成本效率。

Conclusion: 超过50%的中等复杂度缺陷可由低成本LLM-based APR技术修复；APR技术间的修复效果差距随故障定位精度降低而增大；GPT-5在复杂缺陷修复数上优于DeepSeek系列，但DeepSeek-V3.2在总修复成本上表现出最佳的成本效率。

Abstract: Background: Software bugs remain a critical challenge in development, necessitating effective Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based APR systems have shown promise, prior studies primarily focus on overall repair effectiveness. The effects of bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and repair cost-effectiveness remain insufficiently explored.
  Aims: This study presents a comprehensive empirical analysis of LLM-based APR, focusing on how repair performance is shaped by bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and costs.
  Method: We evaluate two APR techniques (ChatRepair and CodeCorrector) using three LLMs (DeepSeek, GPT, and Llama), and examine their performance across diverse levels of bug complexity and localization strategies through a multi-dimensional empirical framework and statistical analysis.
  Results: Although structurally complex bugs and imprecise fault localization make repair more challenging, LLM-based APR techniques still achieve competitive repair effectiveness. Imprecise fault localization can substantially enlarge the performance gap between APR techniques. Furthermore, higher-cost LLMs and stronger reasoning settings do not consistently yield better cost-efficiency, revealing a nontrivial trade-off between repair effectiveness and computational cost.
  Conclusions: Over 50% of moderately complex bugs can be repaired by low-cost LLM-based APR techniques. The repair effectiveness gap between APR techniques becomes larger as fault localization becomes less precise. GPT-5 repairs 7 and 39 more complex bugs than DeepSeek-V4-pro and DeepSeek-V3.2, respectively; whereas the total repair cost of DeepSeek-V3.2 shows the best cost-efficiency performance.

</details>


### [108] [DepWareTrans: Dependency-Aware Incremental Repository Migration across Co-executable Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14128)
*Sivajeet Chand,Alexander Pretschner,Steve Haupt,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种依赖感知的增量迁移框架，将代码翻译的单位从单个文件提升为依赖一致的批次，通过构建依赖图、分组相互依赖的文件并进行批处理翻译，在Java-Kotlin、Java-Scala和C#-F#等多种语言对上实现了100%的编译和测试成功率，而文件级方法在工业系统上仅为38.16%和9.39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的代码翻译方法主要停留在文件级别，无法扩展到具有复杂跨文件依赖关系的代码库。作者在工业实践中迁移一个Java到Kotlin的生产仓库时，发现文件级方法产生碎片化结果且无法实现端到端的正确性，根本原因在于依赖不一致性。

Method: 提出依赖感知的增量迁移框架：首先构建依赖图，然后将相互依赖的文件分组为依赖一致的批次，最后以编译和测试驱动的方式对这些批次进行迭代验证和翻译。该方法将翻译的基本单位从单个文件提升到批次级别。

Result: 在51K行代码的工业系统STAR以及多个开源仓库上进行了评估，覆盖Java-Kotlin、Java-Scala和C#-F#三种可互操作语言对。文件级方法在STAR仓库上编译成功率为38.16%、测试成功率为9.39%，而所提方法在所有评估场景中均达到100%的编译和测试成功率，并在少量迭代内收敛。

Conclusion: 依赖感知的批处理方式能够有效提升仓库级代码翻译的可扩展性和可靠性，解决了文件级方法因跨文件依赖不一致而失败的核心问题。

Abstract: Repository-level code translation is critical for modernizing legacy systems, yet existing approaches based on large language models (LLMs) operate at the file level and fail to scale to codebases with complex inter-file dependencies. This limitation is evident in our industrial setting, where we aim to migrate a production repository (STAR) from Java to Kotlin, but file-level approaches produce fragmented results and fail to achieve end-to-end correctness. In this paper, we show that the primary cause of failure at the repository level is dependency inconsistency. Through an empirical study on open-source and industrial systems, we find that most errors arise from unresolved cross-file dependencies that cannot be effectively addressed by iterative feedback alone. We propose a dependency-aware incremental migration framework that elevates the unit of translation from individual files to dependency-consistent batches. Our approach constructs a dependency graph, groups interdependent files, and performs batched translation with iterative compile- and test-driven validation. We evaluate our method on a 51K line of code (LOC) industrial system and multiple repositories across interoperable language pairs (Java-Kotlin, Java-Scala, and C#-F#). On the STAR repository, file-level approaches achieve 38.16% compilation and 9.39% test success, whereas our approach achieves 100% compilation and test success across the evaluated settings, converging within a small number of iterations. These results show that dependency-aware batching improves scalability and reliability in repository-level code translation.

</details>


### [109] [LegacyWorld: Atomicity-Aware Evaluation of GUI Agents for Legacy Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14131)
*Thilo Reintjes,Sivajeet Chand,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Alexander Pretschner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对遗留企业系统自动化改造中多模态LLM智能体（computer-use agents）的原子性问题进行了预部署评估研究。研究构建了28个Windows GUI工作流基准，对比专家编写提示与屏幕录制生成提示，发现任务完成率、安全失败率和非原子副作用是三种不同的操作特征，提出工作流捕获、状态验证器和原子性感知验收测试应作为AI遗留工作流自动化的首要需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留及类遗留企业系统难以现代化，因为关键工作流仅暴露有限的可编程接口，仍需手动GUI交互。开发legacy-use框架时，领域专家发现有状态工作流中，智能体运行失败仍可能在业务或医疗记录中留下持久的无效变更，因此需要原子性评估：运行要么正确完成工作流，要么失败且不产生意外持久副作用。

Method: 构建由领域专家指导的28个Windows GUI工作流基准，每个工作流指定初始状态、目标状态和任务特定验证器。对比专家精心编写的提示与从专家黄金路径屏幕录制生成的提示，在六个托管的computer-use agents上进行系统评估。

Result: 在六个托管的computer-use agents上的结果显示，有用完成率、安全失败率和非原子性副作用是三种截然不同的操作特征。说明智能体在这些维度上表现独立，某个维度表现好不代表其他维度也表现好。

Conclusion: 工作流捕获、状态验证器以及原子性感知的验收测试应被作为基于AI的遗留工作流自动化的第一类需求（first-class requirements）加以对待，以确保自动化方案在实际部署中的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Legacy and legacy-like enterprise systems often remain difficult to modernize because critical workflows expose limited programmable interfaces and still require manual GUI interaction. This paper reports a pre-deployment evaluation study motivated by the development of legacy-use, an industry-oriented framework for automating such workflows with multimodal LLM agents. During framework development, domain experts helped identify stateful workflows where successful demos are not sufficient: a failed agent run may still leave persistent invalid changes in business or healthcare records. We therefore evaluate computer-use agents using atomicity: a run should either complete the intended workflow correctly or fail without unintended persistent side effects. We construct a domain-expert-informed benchmark of 28 Windows GUI workflows, each specified with an initial state, goal state, and task-specific validator. We compare expert-crafted prompts with prompts generated from screen recordings of expert golden-path executions. Across six hosted computer-use agents, our results show that useful completion, safe failure, and non-atomic side effects are distinct operational profiles. We conclude that workflow capture, state validators, and atomicity-aware acceptance tests should be first-class requirements for AI-based legacy workflow automation.

</details>


### [110] [Breaking Models to Test the Judge: A Mutation Testing Approach for Semantic Evaluators of Domain Class Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14315)
*Kevin Delcourt,Meriem Ben Chaaben,Abdelhamid Rouatbi,Luciano Marchezan,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将变异测试作为评估语义判断器（如基于LLM的判断器）的可扩展替代方案，通过向领域类图中注入受控语义缺陷来评估候选判断器的缺陷检测能力，实验表明该方法与人工评估结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中许多语义建模任务缺乏唯一的标准答案，人工判断既昂贵又主观，需要一种可扩展的替代方案来评估语义判断器的质量。

Method: 提出变异测试方法：从PlantUML类图与文本系统描述对出发，定义11种变异算子（如删除类）注入受控语义缺陷生成错误变体，然后评估候选判断器检测注入缺陷的能力。

Result: 在六种判断器配置（三种LLM和两种提示变体）下，自动化变异测试方法在识别性能更优配置方面与人工评估结果基本一致。

Conclusion: 变异测试可以作为分析语义判断器的可扩展代理方法，为替代昂贵的人工评估提供了可行的自动化途径。

Abstract: In software engineering, many semantic modeling tasks lack a unique ground truth, as human judgments are both costly and subjective. This paper explores mutation testing as a scalable alternative for evaluating semantic judges (e.g., LLM-based) of models. We propose a mutation testing approach in which controlled semantic defects are injected into domain class diagrams. Starting from pairs of PlantUML class diagrams and textual system descriptions, we apply mutation operators (e.g., removing a class) to generate faulty variants. A candidate judge is then evaluated based on its ability to detect the injected defects. We define 11 mutation operators for the task of comparing a domain class diagram against a textual description and evaluate the proposed approach against a conventional manual assessment of judgment validity. Across six judge configurations (three LLMs and two prompt variants), the automated mutation testing approach is largely consistent with the manual assessment in identifying the better-performing configurations. The results suggest that mutation testing may serve as a scalable proxy for analyzing semantic judges.

</details>


### [111] [ATLAS: Discovering Agent Strategies through LLM-Guided Abstraction and Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito,Ezio Bartocci,Bettina Könighofer,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS方法，通过将LLM代理的轨迹抽象与自动机学习相结合，从代理与环境交互的轨迹中恢复可解释的有限状态行为模型，实现对代理策略的系统性理解和分析，并在渗透测试场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的代理在软件测试和网络安全等复杂任务中应用日益广泛，但现有评估方法主要关注任务成功率和执行轨迹，难以揭示代理采用的行为策略，导致其行为难以理解、解释和分析。

Method: ATLAS方法结合轨迹抽象与自动机学习技术，从代理轨迹中推断有限状态模型，捕获代理与环境交互的行为策略。该模型支持人工可解释性分析，并可用于模型指导的探索、审计以及从强大模型到紧凑模型的知识迁移。

Result: 将ATLAS应用于LLM驱动的渗透测试代理生成的轨迹，所获得的行为模型揭示了难以从原始执行轨迹中识别的高层行为策略。在包含12台易受攻击机器的案例研究中，展示了模型变换可生成代理行为的简明解释，并验证了符号模型驱动的知识迁移能力。

Conclusion: ATLAS展示了模型驱动工程的新机遇：将代理轨迹转化为显式行为模型，使原本不透明的AI代理能够被系统地理解和分析，为可解释性、审计和模型间知识迁移提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for complex tasks such as software testing and cybersecurity assessment. While these agents demonstrate impressive capabilities, their behavior is difficult to understand, explain, and analyze. Existing evaluations focus mainly on task success and execution traces, offering limited insight into the strategies employed by the agent. We present ATLAS (Automata Learning for Agent Trajectory Analysis and Strategy Discovery), an approach for recovering interpretable behavioral models from agent trajectories. ATLAS combines trace abstraction with automata learning to infer finite-state models that capture observed agent-environment interaction strategies. These models provide human-interpretable insights and support automated analyses of recurring behaviors, decision points, successful task-completion paths, and failure loops. As a proof of concept, we apply ATLAS to trajectories generated by an LLM-based penetration-testing agent. The resulting models expose high-level behavioral strategies for exploiting vulnerable machines that are difficult to identify from raw execution traces alone. We discuss how learned behavioral models can support explainability, model-guided exploration, auditing, and analysis of agentic systems. We further demonstrate symbolic model-based knowledge transfer from powerful frontier models to compact language models. In addition, we show how model transformations can derive concise explanations of agent behavior in a penetration-testing case study comprising 12 vulnerable machines. ATLAS highlights a new opportunity for model-driven engineering: transforming agent trajectories into explicit behavioral models that enable systematic understanding and analysis of otherwise opaque AI agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] ["I Thought You Were The Uncensored Place": Norms, Rules, and Moderation in AI-Generated Sexual Content Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13659)
*Lucy Qin,Jaron Mink,Elissa M. Redmiles*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过访谈24位大型AI生成性内容（AIG-SC）在线社区的成员和版主，探讨了社区治理如何在防止滥用与支持自由表达之间取得平衡，揭示了社区规范、内容审核机制与滥用行为之间的复杂张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成性内容的快速增长，相关在线社区不断涌现，但如何通过社区治理防止滥用内容（如AI生成的CSAM）同时保障创作者自由表达，缺乏深入理解。

Method: 对24位拥有1万+成员的大型AIG-SC在线社区的成员和版主进行深度访谈，覆盖社区形成动因、隐含规范、明文规则及审核实践等多维度分析。

Result: 研究发现：多数创作者和社区虽有个人反虐待边界，但创建任何AI生成性内容的建议和资源对所有用户开放；反审查和非评判性规范使版主只能依据法律和平台服务条款来正当化其审核行为，留下了滥用空间。

Conclusion: 技术治理、社区治理和法律治理的多层次协同可能最有效地减少滥用内容的产生，需要重新审视社区价值观与审核机制之间的张力。

Abstract: As AI-generated sexual content (AIG-SC) is increasingly produced, online communities have emerged to support creators' needs. To understand whether and how community governance attempts work to prevent abuse while supporting free expression, we interviewed 24 members and moderators of large AIG-SC online communities (10,000+ members) with stated rules against creating and sharing abusive content (e.g., AI-generated CSAM). Through in-depth interviews, we offer insight into: (1) how and why these communities form; (2) implicit community norms; (3) explicitly stated rules---and their operationalization via content moderation; and (4) tensions between community values and moderation that leave space for abusive behavior.
  Our findings reveal a complex picture: while many creators and communities have personal boundaries against abuse, advice and resources for creating any form of AI-generated sexual content are accessible to users regardless of their intentions. Further complicating community moderation are norms that center anti-censorship and non-judgment, which leave moderators to justify their actions using the limits of the law and terms of service. We end by reflecting on the ways in which technical, community, and legal governance may most effectively mitigate the production of abusive content.

</details>


### [113] [Weird Machines in Transport Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13685)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty,Jonathan Takeshita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次将"怪异机器"理论扩展到TLS握手协议及其两大主流实现（OpenSSL和BoringSSL），证明合法的TLS原语（会话缓存、重协商逻辑、扩展解析、证书验证）可以组合成图灵完备的计算系统，并通过两个实际演示（哨兵防御系统和认证绕过攻击）验证了这一理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 怪异机器理论已在软件系统（x86指令、ELF元数据、页表）和网络物理系统（工业控制网络）中得到广泛研究，但尚未被系统地应用于TLS握手协议领域。作者希望将该理论扩展到一个新的域，并揭示TLS实现中隐藏的、可被利用的潜在计算能力。

Method: 作者形式化地提出了"信任致动"（trust actuation）概念，将TLS中的计算与认证/信任决策相耦合。他们论证了只要TLS实现具备会话存储、序列计数器上的算术运算、握手状态上的条件分支以及通过恢复或重试循环的迭代能力，就能满足任意计算的条件。随后基于真实OpenSSL代码路径构建了两个演示系统进行验证。

Result: 两个工作演示均在Docker中针对真实服务器和客户端二进制运行成功：1）哨兵系统利用标准TLS原语组合成检测异常握手行为的防御机制；2）认证绕过攻击通过中间连接重协商击败密码强度策略检查，无需任何内存损坏或外部恶意软件。

Conclusion: TLS握手机制中存在可被组合为图灵完备系统的"怪异机器"，其计算能力与认证和信任决策耦合（即信任致动）。这类系统既能用于防御（如哨兵检测）也能被用于攻击（如认证绕过），且无需内存破坏即可实现，对TLS协议安全性提出了新的理论挑战与实践警示。

Abstract: Weird machines are latent computational capabilities that emerge from the composition of architectural components. Prior work has studied this phenomenon extensively in software systems, including x86 instructions, ELF metadata, and page tables, and more recently in cyber-physical systems such as industrial control networks. This paper extends weird machine theory to a new domain: the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake and its two dominant implementations, OpenSSL and BoringSSL.
  We show that legitimate TLS primitives, including session cache entries, renegotiation logic, extension parsing, and certificate verification steps, compose into Turing-complete systems whose computation is coupled to authentication and trust decisions rather than physical actuation. We formalize this coupling, which we call trust actuation, and argue that any TLS implementation providing session storage, arithmetic on sequence counters, conditional branching on handshake state, and iteration through resumption or retry loops satisfies the conditions for arbitrary computation.
  We validate this theory with two working demonstrations built on real OpenSSL code paths. The first, a sentinel system, composes standard TLS primitives into a defensive mechanism that detects anomalous handshake behavior. The second, an authentication bypass, composes the same class of primitives into an attack that defeats a cipher-strength policy check through mid-connection renegotiation, without any memory corruption or external malware. Both demonstrations run against real server and client binaries in Docker.

</details>


### [114] [A Reproducibility Protocol for Cross-Implementation Evaluation of Post-Quantum ACVP Test Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13784)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究定义了产品中立的可复现性协议，对三个公开的NIST ML-KEM实现（@noble/post-quantum 0.7.0、liboqs 0.16.0、Go 1.26.4）进行了验证。2,160条基础记录全部符合预期：1,650条可执行评估与NIST预言机匹配，510条不支持记录与Go的能力边界匹配；所有可执行重叠部分的配对一致性完全通过。三个冻结控制组均产生预定结果，无失败或错误发生，建立了有界的作者运行可复现性，但揭示了公共ML-KEM包在确定性和验证测试表面上的实质性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学标准的不同独立实现应当产生相同的已知答案结果，但只有当语料库、修订版本、公共接口、排除项和证据都被精确陈述时，一致性才有意义。现有研究缺乏产品中立的可复现性协议来严格验证ML-KEM实现之间的一致性，也未能暴露公共实现之间在测试表面上的差异。

Method: 定义协议v2，冻结各提供商特定能力，对称应用单一验证错误分类法，保留所有选定案例，并分离字节、验证结论、不支持操作和适配器错误。对三个ML-KEM实现进行源码构建实验，重复三次，计算所需笛卡尔积共2,160条基础记录，并对三个冻结控制组分别进行字节比较、验证结论比较和畸形响应错误分离测试。

Result: 1,650条声明可执行的评估全部与NIST预言机匹配，510条不支持记录与Go预声明能力边界匹配；noble-liboqs配对720/720完全一致，每个Go配对210/210完全一致；keyGen-ek投影诊断150/150匹配。三个冻结控制组均产生精确的预定结果，无基础失败、适配器错误或状态不稳定。

Conclusion: 实验证据确立了有界的作者运行可复现性，但独立外部复现尚未观察到。结果不构成认证、穷举正确性、侧信道抗性、安全集成或生产保障。研究暴露了一个实际标准缺口：公共ML-KEM包在确定性和验证测试表面上存在实质性差异。

Abstract: Independent implementations of a cryptographic standard should reproduce the same known-answer results, yet agreement is meaningful only when the corpus, revisions, public interfaces, exclusions, and evidence are precisely stated. This study defines a product-neutral reproducibility protocol for three public implementations of NIST ML-KEM against a pinned public Automated Cryptographic Validation Protocol corpus. Protocol v2 freezes provider-specific capabilities, applies one validation-error taxonomy symmetrically, preserves every selected case, and separates bytes, validation verdicts, unsupported operations, and adapter errors. The source-built experiment evaluated @noble/post-quantum 0.7.0, liboqs 0.16.0, and Go 1.26.4. Across three repetitions, the required Cartesian product comprised 2,160 base records: all 1,650 declared executable evaluations matched the NIST oracle, and all 510 unsupported records matched Go's predeclared capability boundary. Pairwise agreement was complete on every executable overlap: 720 of 720 noble-liboqs records and 210 of 210 records for each Go pairing. A separate keyGen-ek-projection diagnostic matched all 150 Go encapsulation-key projections without counting them as full key generation. Three frozen controls independently exercised byte comparison, verdict comparison, and malformed-response error separation; each produced its exact predeclared outcome. No base failure, adapter error, or status instability occurred. The evidence establishes bounded author-run repeatability and exposes a practical standards gap: public ML-KEM packages provide materially different deterministic and validation-test surfaces. Independent external reproduction remains unobserved. The results do not establish certification, exhaustive correctness, side-channel resistance, secure integration, or production assurance.

</details>


### [115] [The ack3 H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset: Audit Scope Across 135 Security Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13792)
*Josef Gattermayer,Jan Kalivoda,Arman Bašović*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集（ack3发布），分析了135起DeFi安全事件中68起可追溯审计历史的事件，发现67.6%的攻击路径（按数量）和94.4%的损失（按金额）发生在所有公开审计范围之外，表明审计范围覆盖度与实际安全事件路径之间缺乏直接对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计标签常被当作项目级安全保障，但实际审计只覆盖特定时间点的特定工件。作者希望厘清审计历史与事件路径范围之间的关系，纠正行业对审计覆盖度的误读。

Method: 基于ack3发布的H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集（覆盖2026年1月1日至6月29日，报告损失9.3986亿美元），对135起事件进行审计历史追踪与攻击路径范围交叉分析，并按数量与损失金额两个维度统计；另通过剔除两起特大事件进行稳健性检验，同时考察审计年龄、时间损失分布与受影响项目类型。

Result: 68起有审计历史的事件中，46条攻击路径在全部公开的审计范围之外，20条在至少一个范围内，2条未解决。范围外路径占数量67.6%、占损失94.4%；剔除两起特大事件后占比降为72.1%，但方向不变。

Conclusion: 项目级审计历史与事件攻击路径范围是两个不同变量，不能将审计标签等同于项目整体或未来安全的保证，行业需重新审视审计覆盖度的表达与解读。

Abstract: Smart-contract audits cover defined artifacts at a specific time, but the label audited is often treated as project-wide assurance. We analyze audit history and incident-path scope across 135 DeFi security incidents using the H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset published by cybersecurity company ack3 (https://ack3.ai), covering 1 January to 29 June 2026. The corpus reports USD 939.86 million in attributed loss. Audit history was identified for 68 incidents. Of these, 46 attack paths were outside all identified public pre-incident audit scopes, 20 were inside at least one scope, and 2 were unresolved. Within this 68-incident subset, outside-scope paths represented 67.6% by count and 94.4% of reported loss. The loss-weighted result was concentrated in two large incidents; excluding both reduced the share to 72.1%, while preserving the direction of the result. We also describe audit age, temporal loss distribution, and affected project types. The results show that project-level audit history and incident-path scope are distinct variables.

</details>


### [116] [Mobile Apps vs. Web Browsers: A User Perception Study with Android Apps and Google Chrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13803)
*Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验和调查，探讨了Android智能手机用户在移动应用与网页浏览器之间选择时对安全、隐私和可用性的感知差异，为开发者优化两大平台提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在使用移动服务时面临在原生应用和网页浏览器之间的选择，但缺乏对两者在安全、隐私和可用性方面差异的系统性认知研究。

Method: 采用实验与问卷调查相结合的方法，以Android智能手机用户为研究对象，收集用户对移动应用和网页浏览器的安全、隐私及可用性感知数据。

Result: 研究识别出影响用户选择移动应用或网页浏览器的关键关注点和偏好因素，并发现了用户对两大平台存在的误解。

Conclusion: 研究成果可为开发者提供有价值的参考，帮助其针对用户关注点和误解，改进移动应用与网页浏览器的安全性、隐私保护和可用性。

Abstract: This study examines user perceptions of mobile applications (apps) versus web browsers for accessing online services, with an emphasis on security, privacy, and usability aspects. Through a combination of an experiment and a survey with Android smartphone users, the research seeks to identify the key concerns and preferences that influence their choice between mobile apps and web browsers. The findings will offer valuable insights for developers to improve the security, privacy, and usability of both platforms by addressing user concerns and misconceptions.

</details>


### [117] [Vaulted Passkeys: A Device-Bound Proposal for Authenticated Credential Export and Import](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13806)
*Pol Henarejos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Vaulted Passkeys架构，通过256位随机密钥保护的AEAD凭证信封，实现设备绑定凭证的安全导出与恢复，弥补了硬件认证器在灾难恢复场景下无法备份原始凭证的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件认证器有意抵抗私钥提取，但在更换设备、灾难恢复和受控迁移场景中需要凭证可移植性。现有做法通过预先注册额外认证器实现冗余而非真正的凭证备份，存在恢复缺口。

Method: 提出Vaulted Passkeys架构：使用随机256位Kvault密钥通过HKDF派生子密钥，结合四种显式AEAD配置文件保护认证的PKV1凭证信封；将注册与导出/导入解耦，区分必需的金库与可选的身份组件，并设计了角色分离的系统模型、线格式和威胁分析。

Result: 原型实现证明了可行性，提供了角色分离系统模型、线格式、威胁分析和可证伪的评估计划，但明确表示该工作既非正式安全证明也非最终标准提案。

Conclusion: Vaulted Passkeys在不过度克隆完整认证器且不向常规桌面软件暴露明文私钥的前提下，实现了设备绑定凭证的安全备份与恢复，为硬件认证器迁移提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Hardware authenticators deliberately resist private-key extraction, yet replacement, disaster recovery, and controlled migration create a legitimate need for portability. Existing guidance for device-bound credentials commonly reduces recovery risk by registering an additional authenticator before failure. That creates an independent credential registration and requires replacement hardware to exist in advance; it is redundancy, not a backup of the original credential. This paper addresses the resulting recovery gap by exporting protected credential state while the source is available and restoring it to hardware acquired later, without cloning a complete authenticator or exposing plaintext private keys to routine desktop software. We propose Vaulted Passkeys, a device-bound architecture in which a random 256-bit Kvault protects authenticated PKV1 credential envelopes through HKDF-separated keys and four explicit AEAD profiles. The design separates enrollment from export/import and the required vault from optional identity. We contribute a role-separated system model, wire format, threat analysis, implementation mapping, and falsifiable evaluation plan. The prototype demonstrates feasibility but is neither a formal security proof nor a proposed final standard.

</details>


### [118] [TLF: Rapid Characterization of RF Transceiver Parameters in Embedded Systems via Bus-Level Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13815)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TLF（收发器提升框架）是一种从微控制器与收发器IC之间的总线级迹线中恢复RF收发器配置和运行时行为的工具，通过有状态协议解码器重建工作参数，并可恢复密钥材料、跳频序列等，在SX1233和SX1276两个平台上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嵌入式无线系统中，微控制器与收发器IC之间的通信携带了关键的RF配置信息，但目前缺乏有效的工具从总线级迹线中自动恢复这些配置和运行时行为，尤其是在没有固件先验知识的情况下。

Method: TLF构建了一个基于收发器寄存器和数据接口的有状态协议解码器，从拦截到的寄存器写入和FIFO传输中重建RF参数和行为。对于通过主机接口加载硬件加密密钥的收发器，可恢复密钥材料；对于跳频固件，可提取信道表、跳频序列和时序。此外，可插拔协议解码器将恢复的FIFO载荷解释为应用PDU。

Result: 在Semtech家族的SX1233（基于固件级FHSS和逐包同步字旋转的UAV C2调制解调器）和SX1276（运行LoRa寄存器叠加的Meshtastic节点）两个目标上进行了评估，单次总线捕获数秒内即可恢复完整的寄存器暴露RF配置（调制、频段规划、相位行为），并在Meshtastic上端到端演示了应用PDU解码。

Conclusion: TLF能够在不了解目标固件的情况下，从单次总线捕获中恢复完整的RF配置，足以配置匹配接收器或开发针对性对策。但其恢复深度受限于收发器寄存器接口，固件层实现的自定义FEC、白化和加密只能作为不透明FIFO载荷观察。该方法需要物理访问或仿真目标硬件。

Abstract: We present TLF (Transceiver Lifter Framework), a tool for recovering RF transceiver configuration and runtime behavior from bus-level traces captured between a microcontroller and its transceiver IC. A stateful protocol decoder, built against the transceiver's register and data interface, reconstructs operating RF parameters and behavior from intercepted register writes and FIFO transfers. For bus-attached transceivers whose hardware-cryptography keys are loaded through the intercepted host interface, key material is also recoverable. Where the firmware drives frequency hopping -- either through a hardware-assisted engine or a custom schedule -- the decoder extracts the channel table, hop sequence, and timing. We evaluate the approach on two targets from different Semtech families: an SX1233-based UAV C2 modem employing firmware-level FHSS with per-packet sync word rotation, and an SX1276-based Meshtastic node exercising the LoRa register overlay. From a single bus capture, processed in seconds, TLF recovers the complete register-exposed RF configuration (modulation, band plan, phase behavior) without prior knowledge of the target firmware -- sufficient to configure a matched receiver or develop targeted countermeasures. Above the chip layer, a pluggable protocol decoder interprets recovered FIFO payloads as application PDUs, demonstrated end-to-end on Meshtastic. Firmware-level cryptographic state remains, as expected, opaque. The approach requires physical access or emulation of the target hardware, and its recovery depth is bounded by the transceiver's register interface: parameters implemented entirely in firmware (custom FEC, whitening, encryption) are observable only as opaque FIFO payloads.

</details>


### [119] [Verified Pythagorean Composition for Adaptive Cryptographic Games: Noise Flooding in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13846)
*Yi Lee,Alexandru Cojocaru,Junyi Liu,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文用机器验证（Rocq和SSProve）形式化了噪声洪泛（noise flooding）对近似同态加密的解密攻击安全性证明，克服了混合论证中损失随查询次数线性增长的问题，通过累积条件KL散度并仅做一次统计距离转换，实现了q查询IND-CPAD安全的平方根损失上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声洪泛是抵御近似同态加密解密攻击的标准防御手段，但其安全性证明对组合方式异常敏感。朴素混合论证会导致安全性损失随查询次数q线性增长，而密码学证明通过累积条件KL散度实现参数关键的平方根损失。此论证的微妙性亟需机器验证以保证其正确性。

Method: 在SSProve语义之上构建一种新的关系式程序逻辑，其毕达哥拉斯式判定（Pythagorean judgment）能够在无需转换为统计距离的情况下组合条件KL预算；同时实现了一个经验证的迹编译器（trace compiler），将局部预言机规则提升到任意自适应程序，仅需一次最终转换。

Result: 对任意近似正确且IND-CPA安全的FHE方案，形式化了q查询IND-CPAD安全性归约，证明了Pr[IND-CPAD_NF^A=1] ≤ β_CPA(B_{A,q}) + √(qn)/(2γ)，其中n为明文维度，γ为洪泛宽度乘子。

Conclusion: 该工作首次以机器验证方式确立了噪声洪泛防御的精确安全界，验证了平方根损失这一参数关键性质的正确性，为近似同态加密的安全性论证提供了可靠的形式化基础。

Abstract: Noise flooding is a standard defense against decryption attacks on approximate homomorphic encryption, but its security proof is unusually sensitive to composition. Replacing each of $q$ adaptive decryption answers with a statistically close simulation and applying an ordinary hybrid argument loses linearly in $q$. The cryptographic proof instead accumulates conditional Kullback-Leibler (KL) costs and converts to statistical distance once, giving the parameter-critical square-root loss.
  We machine-check this argument using Rocq and SSProve. Given any fully homomorphic encryption scheme that is approximately correct and IND-CPA secure, we formalize a reduction for every $q$-query IND-CPAD adversary and prove \[
  \Pr[\mathsf{IND\text{-}CPAD}_{\mathsf{NF}}^{\mathcal A}=1]
  \leq β_{\mathsf{CPA}}(\mathcal B_{\mathcal A,q})
  + \frac{\sqrt{qn}}{2γ}. \] where $n$ is the plaintext dimension and $γ$ is the flooding-width multiplier. Our proof constructs a new relational program logic over SSProve semantics. Its Pythagorean judgment composes conditional KL budgets without converting them to statistical distance, and a verified trace compiler lifts a local oracle rule to arbitrary adaptive programs with a single final conversion.

</details>


### [120] [CipherSight: Robust Website Fingerprinting via Record-Resource Semantic Supervision under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13905)
*Runhan Song,Qiqi Liu,Chuanzhou Pan,Zhenquan Ding,Youquan Xian,Chongru Fan,Lei Cui,Wei Wang,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CipherSight，一种基于TLS记录的层次化框架，用于鲁棒的HTTPS网站指纹识别（WF）。与现有依赖TCP包序列的方法不同，CipherSight从TLS记录中联合编码多级记录属性，通过层次化架构捕获流内依赖和流间交互，并结合掩码记录建模和结构感知目标与语义蒸馏学习鲁棒表征。实验表明其在封闭世界2000+网站类别中达到95.41%准确率，在时间和地理漂移下保持90%以上准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTTPS网站指纹识别方法主要从原始TCP包序列中学习，容易受传输层人工产物影响，难以捕获稳定且泛化的网站表征。实际部署中面临时间和地理变化导致的分布外（OOD）问题，以及开放世界场景中大量未见网站，导致性能显著下降。

Method: 提出TLS记录级层次化框架。核心组件包括：(1) 从TLS记录而非TCP包序列学习网站表征，联合编码多个记录级属性；(2) 层次化架构同时捕获TLS记录间的流内依赖和并发流间的流间交互；(3) 掩码记录建模（MRM）任务学习上下文流量语义；(4) 利用细粒度的记录-资源注释作为特权监督，通过结构感知目标和语义蒸馏增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在封闭世界设定下，超过2000个网站类别中达到95.41%的准确率；在时间和地理漂移场景下保持90%以上的准确率，一致优于所有评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: CipherSight验证了TLS记录级表征相比TCP包序列能更稳定地捕获网站结构模式，层次化建模结合自监督与特权监督是应对实际部署中分布漂移的有效方案。该方法为HTTPS网站指纹识别在真实场景中的鲁棒部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: HTTPS website fingerprinting (WF) aims to identify visited websites from metadata observable in encrypted traffic. However, real-world deployments introduce a significant out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by temporal and geographic changes, while previously unseen websites are common in open-world scenarios. Existing methods primarily learn from raw TCP packet sequences and struggle to capture stable and generalizable website representations, resulting in performance degradation under practical conditions.
  We propose CipherSight, a TLS-record-based hierarchical framework for robust HTTPS WF. Unlike existing approaches that rely on TCP packet sequences and are sensitive to transport-layer artifacts, CipherSight learns website representations from TLS records by jointly encoding multiple record-level attributes. It introduces a hierarchical architecture that captures both intra-flow dependencies among TLS records and inter-flow interactions across concurrent flows, enabling the model to exploit structural patterns in HTTPS traffic. Besides, to learn robust representations, CipherSight employs a masked record modeling (MRM) task to capture contextual traffic semantics and leverages fine-grained record-resource annotations as privileged supervision through structure-aware objectives and semantic distillation. Experiments show that CipherSight achieves 95.41% accuracy across more than 2,000 website classes in the closed-world setting and maintains over 90% accuracy under both temporal and geographic drift, consistently outperforming all evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [121] [Characterizing the Variance Envelope: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Spectre Telemetry Across Architectures and Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13920)
*Jaya Keshava Chandra Kotha,Jean-Luc Gaudiot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了硬件性能计数器（HPC）在真实环境下检测Spectre等硬件攻击的脆弱性。通过跨Intel、ARM、AMD架构的大规模实验，证明了攻击签名会因背景噪声、攻击变体和流量速度控制而大幅偏移，静态检测模型无法胜任真实世界的检测任务，需要架构感知的自适应监控方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的HPC攻击检测方法仅在受控环境中有效，静态模型面对真实世界中的背景系统噪声、多样攻击变体和对抗性流量控制时会失效，急需系统性刻画攻击签名在实际执行环境中的变化范围。

Method: 作者定义了"方差包络"（variance envelope）概念来描述攻击签名的全部变化范围，并在Intel、ARM、AMD三大架构上构建了涵盖三种攻击变体、四种流量控制模式和四种背景噪声条件的广泛实验矩阵进行系统性评估。

Result: 实验证明HPC签名高度脆弱，极易被执行环境扭曲；同时发现了AMD Jaguar微架构上Prime+Probe攻击的硬件级持久失效这一关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 可靠的在运行时检测需要架构感知的适应性监控方案，而非静态检测模型。

Abstract: Hardware attacks like Spectre exploit built-in processor vulnerabilities, leaving anomalous footprints in Hardware Performance Counter (HPC) metrics. While machine learning can detect these footprints in controlled settings, static models fail in the real world when confronted with background system noise, diverse attack variants, and adversarial traffic pacing. To close this gap, this paper characterizes the "variance envelope"-the full range of how attack signatures shift- across Intel, ARM, and AMD architectures. We evaluate an extensive experimental matrix encompassing three attack variants, four pacing modes, and four background-noise conditions. Our analysis proves that HPC signatures are highly fragile and easily warped by their execution environment. Furthermore, we expose a critical microarchitectural bottleneck: the persistent, hardware-level failure of Prime+Probe attacks on the AMD Jaguar. Ultimately, this comprehensive characterization demonstrates that reliable runtime detection requires architecture-aware, adaptive monitoring rather than static models.

</details>


### [122] [CoSA: Context-Aware Severity Assessment via Context Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13928)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Yikun Li,Chengran Yang,Ting Zhang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSA，一种基于上下文的漏洞严重性评估方法，通过构建代码属性图并采用两阶段仓库剪枝策略（静态剪枝+智能体LLM引导剪枝），将仓库级证据提炼为CVSS指标级摘要，输入轻量级Transformer预测器进行CVSS基础指标推断。在包含6,816个标注实例、覆盖90种CWE类型的仓库级数据集上，CoSA相比最优基线在准确率和Macro-F1上分别提升14.4%和15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动评估CVSS基础指标耗时费力，现有自动化方法难以捕获评估CVSS指标所需的仓库级证据，且相关证据分散在仓库各处并伴随大量噪声干扰。

Method: CoSA首先构建代码属性图（CPG），然后采用两阶段仓库剪枝策略：第一阶段为轻量级静态剪枝以保留结构上邻近的上下文；第二阶段利用智能体大语言模型（LLM）引导剪枝以保留与CVSS相关的上下文并收集支持证据。随后LLM将检索到的仓库上下文整合为紧凑的、按CVSS指标分类的文本摘要，最后输入轻量级Transformer预测器进行严重性评估。

Result: 在真实世界漏洞数据上的实验表明，CoSA一致优于函数级别基线和纯LLM基线，预测准确率提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%。

Conclusion: 显式的、面向指标的仓库上下文检索对于实践可靠且实用的自动化严重性评估至关重要，CoSA验证了该路径的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability severity assessment is essential for prioritizing remediation, yet manually assessing Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) base metrics remains labor-intensive. Existing automated approaches often fail to capture the repository-level evidence required for assessing many CVSS base metrics. Such repository-aware assessment is challenging because relevant evidence is scattered across the entire repository under heavy noise.
  To address these challenges, we present CoSA, a Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment approach that infers CVSS base metrics from repository artifacts. CoSA constructs a code property graph (CPG) and applies a two-stage repository-pruning strategy: lightweight static pruning to preserve structurally proximal context, followed by an agentic large language model (LLM)-guided pruning step to retain CVSS-relevant context while collecting supporting evidence. The LLM then consolidates the retrieved repository context into compact, CVSS metric-wise textual summaries, which are fed into a lightweight transformer predictor. We also construct a higher-quality repository-level dataset comprising 6,816 CVSS labeled instances spanning 90 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) types.
  Experiments on real-world vulnerabilities show that CoSA consistently outperforms function-level and pure-LLM baselines. It improves prediction accuracy by 14.4% and Macro-F1 by 15.3% over the best-performing baseline, suggesting that explicit, metric-oriented repository context retrieval is crucial for practical and reliable automated severity assessment.

</details>


### [123] [Extracting and Verifying Illicit Bitcoin Addresses from Underground Forum Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13930)
*Abdoul Nasser Hassane Amadou,Arnaud Legout,Imane Fouad,Konstantin Avrachenkov,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的流水线，从地下论坛HackForums中构建带证据支持的比特币非法地址标签数据集，包含2,438个经人工验证的非法地址（2010-2024年），覆盖12类网络犯罪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有比特币标签数据集来源多样（社区报告、区块链启发式、事件收集等），但构建方法难以复现，且缺乏地址直接参与非法活动的充分证据，影响研究的可靠性和可验证性。

Method: 结合LLM辅助筛选、专家审查和链上验证三个环节，从HackForums论坛15年的历史活动中识别与非法交易明确关联的比特币地址，每个标签附带地下讨论的上下文证据并经过链上验证。

Result: 构建了包含2,438个手动验证的非法比特币地址数据集，时间跨度为2010-2024年，覆盖12类网络犯罪类别，并公开了数据集、时间元数据和完整提取流水线。

Conclusion: 该工作为加密货币相关网络犯罪研究提供了可复现、带证据支持的高质量标注数据集，弥补了现有数据集在可复现性和证据充分性方面的不足。

Abstract: Existing labeled Bitcoin datasets are largely derived from community-reported abuse, blockchain heuristics, incident-specific collections, or proprietary labeling processes. Their construction methods are rarely publicly reproducible and often provide limited evidence that an address was directly involved in illicit activity. We present a reproducible pipeline for constructing evidence-backed Bitcoin labels from HackForums, an underground cybercrime forum with fifteen years of archived activity. The pipeline combines LLM-assisted screening, expert review, and on-chain validation to identify Bitcoin addresses explicitly associated with illicit transactions discussed on the forum. Each released label is supported by contextual evidence from underground discussions and validated on-chain. The resulting dataset contains 2,438 manually verified illicit Bitcoin addresses spanning 2010-2024 and twelve cybercrime categories assigned during LLM screening. We release the dataset, temporal metadata, and the complete extraction pipeline to support reproducible research on cryptocurrency-facilitated cybercrime.

</details>


### [124] [Vectorized SQIsign Implementation Using AVX-512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13948)
*Weize Wang,Chutong Wang,Yu Wu,Qifan Xue,Jieyu Zheng,Yunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于AVX-512 IFMA指令集的SQIsign完整向量化实现，通过系统重构素数域与扩域算术、椭圆曲线操作及配对计算，在NIST安全级别I下实现签名2.69倍、验证3.18倍的性能提升，并论证了该技术在AVX10架构下的长期适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQIsign是NIST后量子密码标准化中唯一基于同源的数字签名方案，具有紧凑的密钥和签名尺寸优势，但其签名过程计算密集，实际部署受到性能瓶颈制约。当前针对该方案的SIMD向量化优化研究尚属空白。

Method: 利用AVX-512 IFMA指令集对整个计算栈进行系统性向量化重设计，涵盖素数域与扩展域算术、批量点倍加与标量乘法等椭圆曲线运算，以及基于立方算术和二维同源评估的配对计算，并与Qlapoti技术相结合。

Result: 与参考实现相比，在NIST安全级别I下实现签名2.69倍和验证3.18倍的加速比，并通过论证AVX10架构（2026年底部署）将标准化AVX-512/IFMA指令，确保优化技术的长期有效性。

Conclusion: SIMD向量化是后量子同源密码方案中关键但尚未充分探索的优化维度，本文的策略具有架构无关性，可为更广泛的同源密码构造提供方法论基础。

Abstract: SQIsign is the sole isogeny-based digital signature scheme submitted to the NIST Post-Quantum Cryptography standardization process, distinguished by its foundation on the hardness of the endomorphism ring problem for supersingular elliptic curves. While offering compact key and signature sizes, SQIsign's practical deployment is hindered by computationally intensive signing procedures. This paper presents the first comprehensive vectorized implementation of SQIsign utilizing the AVX-512 Integer Fused Multiply-Add (IFMA) instruction set architecture. By systematically redesigning the computational stack---encompassing prime-field and extension-field arithmetic, elliptic curve operations including batched point doubling and scalar multiplication, as well as pairing computations via cubical arithmetic and two-dimensional isogeny evaluations---we achieve substantial performance improvements over the reference implementation. When combined with Qlapoti technology, our implementation attains a $2.69\times$ speedup for signing and a $3.18\times$ improvement for verification at NIST security level I.
  Contrary to misconceptions regarding the obsolescence of AVX-512, we emphasize that Intel's AVX10 instruction set architecture (revision 10.2, scheduled for widespread deployment in late 2026) will standardize AVX-512 capabilities---including IFMA instructions---across both performance and efficiency cores, ensuring long-term viability of these optimization techniques. Furthermore, our vectorization strategies are architecture-agnostic and provide a methodological foundation applicable to broader isogeny-based cryptographic constructions. This work demonstrates that SIMD vectorization represents a critical yet underexplored optimization dimension for post-quantum isogeny-based schemes, independent of recent algorithmic advances.

</details>


### [125] [Structural Leakage in Graph Encryption: Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13981)
*Hua Shen,Renzhi Chen,Ge Wu,Willy Susilo,Jing Chen,Mingwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文全面分析了图加密方案（GES）中单对最短路径（SPSP）查询的结构性泄漏问题，揭示了PathGES方案在重轻分解（HLD）中存在严重不平衡、超过99%的令牌-路径映射为一对一关系，从而易受攻击；并提出了Fragment Tree攻击可恢复查询内容（稀疏图上精确恢复率达10.24%）；同时引入了增强方案BlindGES，通过Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引，将一对一映射降至20%以下、设置时间削减50%、存储开销减少32%、路径长度泄漏控制在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图加密方案支持安全外包图数据及高效查询，但单对最短路径查询存在结构性泄漏问题，现有方案PathGES虽声称能抵抗查询恢复攻击，实际在真实数据集上仍存在严重的安全隐患，亟需系统性分析和更安全的防御方案。

Method: 采用综合分析框架，结合两项近期工作：首先对PathGES的HLD分解和规范片段编码进行结构性泄漏分析；其次提出Fragment Tree攻击利用结构弱点恢复查询内容；最后设计BlindGES方案，引入Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引来减少一对一映射并降低各类开销。

Result: PathGES在真实数据集上超过99%的令牌-路径映射为一对一关系；Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上实现最高10.24%的精确恢复；BlindGES将一对一映射降至20%以下、设置时间减少50%、存储开销减少32%、路径长度泄漏限制在1%以内，并在七个真实数据集上完成实验验证。

Conclusion: 研究表明现有GES方案在SPSP查询中存在显著的结构性泄漏，所提出的攻击方法有效验证了这些弱点，而BlindGES作为增强方案在安全性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，为图加密查询的安全性设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Graph encryption schemes (GES) enable secure outsourcing of graph data while supporting efficient queries. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of structural leakage in GES for single-pair shortest path (SPSP) queries, integrating findings from two recent works. First, we analyze PathGES, a scheme designed to resist query recovery attacks through heavy-light decomposition (HLD) and canonical fragment encoding. Our analysis reveals that PathGES suffers from significant imbalances in HLD decomposition, with over 99% of token-path mappings being one-to-one on real-world datasets, enabling both the Falzon-Paterson attack and side-channel inference of path lengths. Second, we present Fragment Tree attack that exploits these structural weaknesses to recover query contents, achieving up to 10.24% exact recovery on sparse graphs. Third, we introduce BlindGES, an enhanced scheme incorporating a Merge-and-Divide mechanism and two-level multimap index that reduces one-to-one mappings to below 20%, cuts setup time by 50%, reduces storage overhead by 32%, and limits path length leakage to under 1%. This report systematically presents attack methodologies, defense mechanisms, security proofs, and experimental evaluations on seven real-world datasets.

</details>


### [126] [P2Skill: Privacy Preserving Skill Distillation for Cloud-Local LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14094)
*Myunghoon Ryu,Geunpyo Park,Sungjoon Lee,XinYu Piao,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出P2Skill，一种基于提示词技能蒸馏的云-本地LLM推理隐私保护方法，让本地小语言模型通过技能提示自主完成分解、PII感知路由、改写和重建，无需隐私微调或学习辅助检测器，在四域基准上取得1.69倍和3.66倍的隐私保护推理质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云-本地LLM推理系统在利用云端大模型推理能力的同时需要保护用户设备上的敏感数据，现有隐私保护方法（提示扰动、实体掩码、模型微调）会扭曲上下文语义或需要额外训练。

Method: P2Skill通过提示词技能蒸馏方法，让本地小语言模型（SLM）遵循技能提示自主完成分解、PII感知路由、改写和重构四个步骤；云端大模型根据执行失败迭代精炼技能，使本地SLM能泛化超越记忆的PII模式，无需隐私专用微调或学习型辅助检测器。

Result: 在四领域基准上，P2Skill的隐私保护推理质量比先前基线提高了1.69倍和3.66倍。

Conclusion: P2Skill验证了提示词技能蒸馏在云-本地LLM隐私推理中的有效性，通过云端迭代精炼实现无训练、可泛化的隐私保护推理能力。

Abstract: Cloud-local LLM inference systems have the potential to use the reasoning capability of large cloud models while protecting sensitive user data on personal devices. Cloud-bound requests must exclude personally identifiable information (PII) to prevent external data leakage. Existing privacy-preserving methods rely on prompt perturbation, entity masking, or model fine-tuning, but these approaches may distort contextual semantics or require additional training. This paper proposes P2Skill, a prompt-based skill distillation method in which a local small language model (SLM) autonomously performs decomposition, PII-aware routing, paraphrasing, and reconstruction by following the skill prompts. Skills are iteratively refined from execution failures by a cloud LLM, enabling the local SLM to generalize beyond memorized PII patterns, and therefore P2Skill requires no privacy-specific fine-tuning or learned auxiliary detectors. Evaluation on a four-domain benchmark shows that P2Skill achieves $1.69\times$ and $3.66\times$ higher privacy-preserved inference quality than previous baselines.

</details>


### [127] [A Hybrid LLM-Based Framework for Automated Security Annotation Generation in Business Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14370)
*Md Kamrul Islam,Tiphaine Henry,Mattia Salnitri,Julius Köpke,Sami Souihi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合框架，结合LLM语义提取、架构约束映射、规则归一化和确定性验证，自动从自然语言安全需求文档中为BPMN流程模型生成SecBPMN2安全注解。在27个流程模型数据集上评估，该方法在结构有效性、模式完整性方面表现优异，精确率远超人工分析（0.58 vs 0.29），召回率相当（0.52 vs 0.50），错误注解减少近50%，且速度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全业务流程建模需要在流程模型中融入安全注解，虽然已有SecBPMN2等BPMN扩展，但从自然语言规范推导准确完整的安全注解仍依赖人工专家，耗时且易出错，缺乏自动化方案。

Method: 构建混合框架，输入BPMN流程模型和安全需求文档，通过四阶段处理：基于大语言模型的语义提取、模式约束映射、规则归一化以及确定性验证，最终自动生成符合SecBPMN2规范的安全注解。

Result: 在27个跨领域流程模型数据集上，系统生成的结构有效注解具有高模式完整性；精确率0.58显著高于人工的0.29，召回率0.52与人工0.50相当，错误或错放注解减少近50%，且注解生成速度远快于人工。

Conclusion: 混合LLM与规则驱动的自动化方法能够在降低建模工作量的同时提升一致性和可靠性，为安全设计BPM提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: The modelling and analysis of secure business processes require the incorporation of security annotations into process models. Although BPMN extensions, including SecBPMN2, exist for this purpose, the derivation of accurate and complete security annotations from natural-language specifications remains a manual, expert-intensive, and error-prone task. This paper presents a hybrid framework that takes a BPMN process model and a security requirements document as input and automatically generates security annotations adhering to the SecBPMN2 specification. The approach combines Large Language Model (LLM)--based semantic extraction with schema-constrained mapping, rule-based normalization, and deterministic validation. The framework is evaluated comprehensively on a curated dataset of 27 process models from various domains. The results indicate that it consistently produces structurally valid SecBPMN2 annotations with high schema completeness. Compared to human security analysts, the system achieves substantially higher precision (0.58 vs. 0.29) while maintaining comparable recall (0.52 vs. 0.50) and reduces erroneous or misplaced annotations by nearly 50%. In addition, annotation generation is significantly faster than manual annotation. These findings demonstrate that hybrid LLM- and rule-based automation can reduce modeling effort while improving consistency and reliability, thereby providing a scalable foundation for security-by-design BPM.

</details>


### [128] [BGA: A noise-immune neural distillation framework for malicious signature extraction in high-entropy encrypted flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14126)
*Sheng Hong,Yixuan Huang,Weiwei Jiang,Junyuan Zhang,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BGA，一种针对高熵TLS 1.3流量的噪声免疫神经蒸馏框架，用于加密威胁情报检测。通过ANOVA解耦高判别性控制面特征，WGAN-GP合成少数类样本解决类别不平衡，结合BiLSTM与自适应门控多头注意力机制抑制加密噪声并放大恶意签名。在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上各项关键指标超过95.2%，噪声注入压力测试中比原始Transformer高出8.57%性能裕度，推理延迟低至0.2820毫秒，具备工业边缘网关实时部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS 1.3高熵流量中加密噪声会稀释攻击特征注意力，导致传统检测方法性能退化；同时工业控制场景中恶意状态命令注入等罕见攻击存在极端类别不平衡问题，亟需一种能够抵御噪声干扰并提升少数类检测能力的框架。

Method: BGA框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 使用ANOVA从随机加密噪声中解耦高判别性的控制面特征（工业设定值）；(2) 集成WGAN-GP生成高保真少数类样本以缓解极端类别不平衡；(3) 采用BiLSTM提取时间依赖，并设计自适应门控多头注意力机制作为神经滤波器，动态抑制加密伪影同时放大恶意签名。

Result: 在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上所有关键指标超过95.2%；MSCI罕见攻击检测召回率提升43.2%；噪声注入压力测试比原始Transformer性能高8.57%；推理延迟0.2820毫秒（ARM理论缩放估计1.6920毫秒），展示实时部署可行性。

Conclusion: BGA为高熵加密流量下的工业威胁检测提供了有效的噪声免疫解决方案，在检测性能、抗噪声鲁棒性和推理效率方面均表现优异，为异构工业边缘网关上的实时部署和未来硬件实现提供了有前景的架构基线。

Abstract: To mitigate attention dilution in high-entropy TLS 1.3 flows, we propose BGA, a noise-immune neural distillation framework for encrypted threat intelligence.The methodology first employs Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) to decouple high-discriminatory control-plane features - specifically industrial setpoints - from stochastic cryptographic noise. To resolve the extreme class imbalance within a corpus of 86,878 flow records, a Wasserstein GAN with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) module, enforcing the 1-Lipschitz constraint, is integrated to synthesize high-fidelity minority samples, elevating the detection recall of rare Malicious State Command Injections(MSCI) attacks by 43.2%. At its core, the BGA architecture integrates Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) for temporal dependency extraction and an Adaptive Gated Multi-Head Attention mechanism. This gated unit functions as a neural filter to dynamically suppress encryption artifacts while amplifying malicious signatures. Extensive evaluations on CIC-IDS-2018 and Edge-IIoT benchmarks demonstrate a performance ceiling exceeding 95.2% across all key metrics. Furthermore, noise-injection stress tests confirm BGAs superior structural resilience with a 8.57% performance margin over vanilla Transformers, while its ultra-low inference latency of 0.2820 ms (estimated 1.6920 ms via theoretical scaling for ARM) indicates a high potential for real-time feasibility on heterogeneous industrial edge gateways, providing a promising architectural baseline for future hardware implementation.

</details>


### [129] [MazeRunner: Nonlinear Task and Clue Orchestration for LLM-driven Black-Box Automated Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14216)
*Zhenyuan Li,Yi Jiang,Junjie Cheng,Yaokun Li,Jing Qiu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MazeRunner是一个基于三智能体任务与线索编排框架的自主渗透测试系统，通过分离全局编排、上下文密集型执行和失败导向审查，实现动作修正、前提恢复、分支切换和长距离线索关联。在10个HTB目标上，以47.7%的子任务完成率优于PentestGPT-V2（36.2%）和Claude Code（34.2%），并在6个目标上获得用户级或更高访问权限（含2个root权限），而基线模型仅在2个目标上获得用户级访问且未获得root权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的渗透测试智能体主要在线性简化场景中执行端到端工作流，而真实黑盒测试本质上是非线性的——攻击图初始未知、需要从环境反馈中增量推断、观察结果可能揭示多个攻击分支、失败往往是模糊的、关键线索可能跨越很长的动作范围，导致现有智能体容易陷入深度优先探索陷阱、错误诊断失败并遗忘先前的证据。

Method: MazeRunner采用三智能体任务与线索编排框架，将全局编排（global orchestration）、上下文密集型执行（context-intensive execution）和失败导向审查（failure-oriented review）分离，同时持续维护任务状态和环境证据。该设计支持动作修订、前提条件恢复、分支切换以及长距离线索关联。

Result: 在10个近期发布的HTB目标上（每次运行限制2000万LLM token且防止目标特定解决方案泄漏），MazeRunner配合Claude Sonnet 4.5完成了47.7%的标注子任务，优于PentestGPT-V2的36.2%和Claude Code的34.2%。在6个目标上获得用户级或更高访问权限，其中2个目标获得root权限；而同模型的基线仅在2个目标上达到用户级访问且未获得root权限。执行轨迹分析表明MazeRunner探索了更多攻击分支并且更高效地获取shell。

Conclusion: MazeRunner通过其任务与线索编排框架有效解决了黑盒渗透测试中的非线性挑战，显著提升了LLM驱动的自主渗透测试能力，在子任务完成率、权限获取等级和攻击分支探索效率方面均优于现有基线方法，证明了分离编排、执行和审查职责在复杂安全审计场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Penetration testing is essential yet resource-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) show promise for automating security auditing, existing agents mainly execute end-to-end workflows in simplified linear scenarios. Real-world black-box testing is fundamentally nonlinear: the attack graph is initially unknown and must be incrementally inferred from environmental feedback. Observations may reveal multiple attack branches, failures are often ambiguous, and critical clues may span long action horizons. Existing agents therefore tend to become trapped in depth-first exploration, misdiagnose failures, and forget prior evidence. We present MazeRunner, an autonomous penetration testing system built on a three-agent task-and-clue orchestration framework. It separates global orchestration, context-intensive execution, and failure-oriented review while persistently maintaining task states and environmental evidence. This design supports action revision, prerequisite recovery, branch switching, and long-range clue correlation. We evaluate MazeRunner on 10 recently released HTB targets, limiting each system-target run to 20 million LLM tokens and preventing target-specific solution leakage. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, MazeRunner completes 47.7% of annotated subtasks, compared with 36.2% for PentestGPT-V2 and 34.2% for Claude Code. It achieves user-level or higher access on six targets, including root access on two; each same-model baseline reaches user-level access on only two targets and never obtains root access. Execution-trace analysis further shows that MazeRunner explores more attack branches and acquires shells more efficiently.

</details>


### [130] [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Principle-Bench基准，覆盖168个加密资产金融推广场景，从准确性、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准性四个维度评估基于原则监管的LLM裁判，并引入Ceca（校准示例聚类评估器）提供精确的反事实归因。研究发现无单一方法能在所有四个维度上胜出，120B模型在关键词填充攻击下准确率从0.74暴跌至0.27，不同模型家族间一致性仅为kappa=0.16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于原则的监管（如"公平、清晰、不误导"）无法简化为二元谓词，LLM裁判正日益被用作替代。然而，这类裁判缺少系统性的评估框架，尤其是在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性和校准性方面存在严重不足。

Method: 构建Principle-Bench基准（168个加密资产金融推广场景，映射到英国FCA两项原则），包含释义扰动、对抗性关键词填充和边界扰动，覆盖四个评估维度。同时提出Ceca（校准示例聚类评估器），实现精确的逐示例反事实归因。

Result: 关键词计数、三种句向量嵌入器、开源权重LLM裁判和校准级联均未能在四个维度上全面占优。120B LLM裁判在良善输入上表现最强，但在关键词填充的Consumer Duty输入上准确率从0.74跌至0.27（"合规表演"）；另一模型家族的裁判在相同分割上一致性仅为kappa=0.16。

Conclusion: 任何用于基于原则监管的部署级LLM裁判，必须报告逐原则的对抗欺骗结果和事后校准，而不能仅报告总体准确率。模型的脆弱性定位于模型本身而非语料库。

Abstract: Principle-based regulation, with evaluative standards such as "fair, clear, and not misleading" or "deliver good outcomes", cannot be reduced to binary predicates, and LLM-as-judge is increasingly used as the substitute. Our position is that any such judge must be evaluated on four axes: accuracy, paraphrase robustness, adversarial robustness, and calibration. We release Principle-Bench, 168 cryptoasset financial-promotion scenarios mapped to two UK FCA principles, with paraphrase, adversarial keyword-stuffing, and boundary perturbations authored under a pre-registered rubric; the first benchmark covering all four axes for principle-based regulation. We also introduce Ceca (Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment): a calibrated, auditable assessor that emits exact per-exemplar counterfactual attributions. Across keyword counting, three sentence-transformer embedders, an open-weight LLM-judge, and a calibrated cascade, no method dominates all four axes. A 120B LLM-judge, strongest on benign inputs, loses 47 accuracy points (0.74 to 0.27) on keyword-stuffed Consumer Duty inputs: "compliance theatre." A second judge from a different model family agrees only at Cohen's kappa = 0.16 on that split, localising the failure to the model rather than the corpus. Any deployment-grade LLM-judge for principle-based regulation must report per-principle adversarial deception and post-hoc calibration alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [131] [STINER: Automated Extraction of Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence from X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14418)
*Yasir Ech-Chammakhy,Oussama Azrara,Jaafar Chbili,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STINER，一个面向社交媒体网络威胁情报提取的专家标注语料库与细粒度实体分类体系，涵盖8种战略实体类型。基准测试显示领域自适应编码器DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1分数，优于通用模型和微调大语言模型。基于此模型进行的2025年上半年欧洲威胁态势分析揭示了对西班牙攻击事件的独特可见性，并能提前捕捉SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体（特别是X平台）已成为网络安全威胁情报的最快来源，常早于正式厂商报告发布漏洞公告。然而社交媒体非正式、不规则的方言使传统命名实体识别（NER）模型难以有效解析，造成自动化防御系统的盲区。

Method: 构建包含2,100条真实警报的高质量专家标注数据集，提出包含威胁行为者、行业部门、地理位置等8种战略实体类型的细粒度分类体系。对9个模型在12种配置下进行基准测试，涵盖通用与领域自适应编码器、开放模式抽取和生成式大语言模型（零样本与微调）。

Result: 领域自适应编码器DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1分数，超越通用基线和微调大语言模型，且推理延迟显著更低。基于STINER-DarkBERT的欧洲威胁态势分析结果与官方报告对齐，同时揭示了SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。

Conclusion: STINER展示了社交媒体驱动的情报提取在战略网络威胁情报中的有效性，表明领域自适应编码器在精度和效率上优于大语言模型方案，并能较早发现新兴威胁信号，为自动化防御系统弥补了关键盲区。

Abstract: Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) focuses on high-level insights, such as identifying targeted industries, attributing attacks to specific ransomware groups, and assessing the scale of data loss. Today, X (formerly Twitter) has become the fastest source for this intelligence, often hosting real-time breach announcements days before formal vendor reports. Converting this raw chatter into actionable intelligence requires navigating a complex linguistic landscape. Conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) models struggle to parse the informal and highly irregular dialect of social media, creating a blind spot for automated defense systems. To address this challenge, we introduce STINER, a taxonomy and expert-annotated corpus for extracting strategic intelligence from social media streams. We construct a high-quality, expert-annotated dataset of 2,100 real-world alerts and propose a granular taxonomy of eight entity types centered on strategic pivots such as Threat Actor, Sector, and Location. We benchmark nine models across 12 evaluated configurations, spanning general-purpose and domain-adapted encoders, open-schema extraction, and generative LLMs in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Domain-adapted encoders such as DarkBERT reach a strict F1-score of 89.33%, outperforming both general-purpose baselines and fine-tuned Large Language Models, which additionally incur substantially higher inference latency. Leveraging STINER-DarkBERT, we conduct a European threat landscape analysis for H1 2025. Our results align with official reporting on major targets while highlighting the distinct visibility profile of attacks in Spain, and illustrate how social-media-driven extraction can surface early signals of the SafePay ransomware campaign prior to its retrospective characterization in vendor threat landscape reports.

</details>


### [132] [Lower Bounds on Black-Box Constructions of Pseudorandom Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14501)
*Bar Alon,Itai Dinur,Muthuramakrishnan Venkitasubramaniam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究从伪随机生成器（PRG）构造伪随机函数（PRF）的黑盒构造复杂度，证明了在完全黑盒构造框架下，非自适应调用PRG的次数至少为Ω(n/log n)（其中n为PRG输入长度），并进一步对输出较长的弱PRF建立了自适应调用次数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GGM构造自1984年提出以来，一直需要ω(log n)次PRG调用，至今没有已知的黑盒构造能用更少的调用次数实现PRF。虽然Beimel等人（CRYPTO 2024）对树形构造证明了GGM的最优性，但能否仅用一次PRG调用构造PRF这一基本问题仍然悬而未决。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，给出更一般的下界结果。

Method: 本文考虑完全黑盒构造框架，要求构造和归约都是黑盒的，且归约与对手的交互次数与对手每次交互中的oracle调用次数无关。作者通过分析归约与敌手之间的交互结构，对非自适应PRG调用建立Ω(n/log n)和Ω(in/log in)的下界，其中in是PRF的输入长度。该不可实现性结果甚至对输出1比特的弱PRF也成立，此时敌手仅能进行i.i.d.均匀随机查询。此外，对于输出足够长的弱PRF，作者还证明了即使在构造允许自适应查询PRG的情况下依然存在下界。

Result: 本文证明了不存在具有o(n/log n)和o(in/log in)次非自适应PRG调用的完全黑盒PRF构造，即使对手被限制为仅做i.i.d.均匀随机查询的一比特输出弱PRF。此外，对输出足够长的弱PRF，建立了自适应PRG调用情况下的下界。

Conclusion: 该结果表明GGM构造在非自适应黑盒构造的意义下是大致最优的（达到n/log n因子的差距），并证实了用单次PRG调用构造PRF这一问题的本质困难所在。作者同时指出，对于输出足够长的弱PRF，即使在自适应调用下，下界依然成立，进一步巩固了黑盒构造的固有复杂性。

Abstract: In their seminal work, Goldreich, Goldwasser, and Micali [CRYPTO 1984] constructed a pseudorandom function (PRF) using a black-box access to a pseudorandom generator (PRG). When combined with Levin's domain extension technique, the GGM construction invokes the PRG $ω(\log n)$ times, where $n$ denotes the input length to the PRG. To this day, no black-box construction achieving fewer calls is known.
  Recently, Beimel, Malkin, and Mazor [CRYPTO 2024] showed that for a certain family of constructions, which they termed \emph{tree constructions}, the GGM construction is optimal. However, the basic challenge of whether a PRF can be built with just \emph{one invocation} of the PRG still remains open.
  In this work, we consider fully black-box constructions of PRFs from PRGs, where both the construction and the reduction are required to be black-box, and the number of interactions the reduction makes with the adversary is independent of the number of oracle calls the adversary makes to its underlying function within each interaction. Our main result shows that no such construction can have $o(n/\log n)$ and $o(\mathsf{in}/\log\mathsf{in})$ \emph{non-adaptive} calls to the PRG, where $\mathsf{in}$ is the input length of the PRF. This impossibility holds even for weak PRFs with one-bit output, where the adversary is restricted to making i.i.d. uniformly random queries. In addition, we prove a lower bound for weak PRFs with sufficiently long outputs that holds even when the construction is allowed to make adaptive queries to the PRG.

</details>


### [133] [Trust Without Boundaries: An Architectural Analysis of Satellite Flight Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14532)
*Jack Vanlyssel,Gruia-Catalin Roman,Kendra Cook,Sazzadur Rahaman,Afsah Anwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了NASA核心飞行软件（cFS）的架构安全弱点，发现其将机载组件视为可信对等体，缺乏内部隔离和访问控制，通过NOS3模拟器中的五个实验验证了单一受损组件可滥用共享权限实施难以与合法行为区分的攻击，并与其他模块化飞行软件框架对比，指出普遍存在的信任假设问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着航天器越来越多地依赖软件且互联程度提高，机载飞行软件已成为重要的安全边界，但流行的飞行软件架构通常将机载组件视为可信对等体，简化了集成却限制了内部隔离和访问控制，亟需审视和加强内部信任边界。

Method: 对NASA的cFS进行分析，考察权威、身份、通信、可观察性和持久性在机载组件间的分布；使用NASA的NOS3模拟器，通过一个恶意机载组件滥用合法架构权限实施五个实验来验证弱点；并与其它模块化飞行软件框架进行比较以识别共同信任假设和架构弱点。

Result: 结果表明，单个受损组件能够利用广泛共享的权威实施攻击，且这些攻击行为难以与合法行为区分。

Conclusion: 提出了架构层面的启示，并讨论了在未来飞行软件系统中加强内部信任边界的机制。

Abstract: As spacecraft become more software-driven and interconnected, onboard flight software is an increasingly important security boundary. Popular flight software architectures often treat onboard components as trusted peers, simplifying integration while limiting internal isolation and access control. We analyze NASA's Core Flight Software (cFS) to examine how authority, identity, communication, observability, and persistence are distributed across onboard components. Using NASA's flight-representative NOS3 simulator, we validate these weaknesses through five experiments implemented with a malicious onboard component that abuses legitimate architectural privileges. We then compare cFS with other modular flight software frameworks to identify recurring trust assumptions and architectural weaknesses. Our results show that a single compromised component can exploit broadly shared authority in ways that are difficult to distinguish from legitimate behavior. We conclude with architectural implications and discuss mechanisms for strengthening internal trust boundaries in future flight software systems.

</details>


### [134] [Finding Vulnerabilities via LLM-Augmented Semantics-Aware Type-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14533)
*Ruizhe Wang,Meng Xu,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SETYPE，一种基于语义感知的类型系统，利用LLM直接从源代码推断和检查类型，通过类型检查失败来检测潜在漏洞。原型PYSETYPE在Python Web应用上达到87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率，发现15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个经开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析漏洞检测依赖专家编码的不安全模式规则，但这种方法侧重于语法模式，忽略了代码中更深层的语义信息（如变量和函数名的含义）。随着软件系统日益复杂，仅用语法规则建模漏洞变得越来越困难。

Method: 提出SETYPE，一种可以从源代码中直接推导的语义感知类型系统，仅基于符号和表达式的自然语言含义。在SETYPE类型系统中，类型推断和类型检查均由LLM执行，类型检查失败即表示存在潜在漏洞。实现原型PYSETYPE用于检测Python Web应用中的漏洞。

Result: 在真实世界的应用上评估，达到87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率。使用PYSETYPE识别出15个潜在的零日漏洞，其中9个被开发者确认为真实漏洞。

Conclusion: 通过利用LLM理解代码的语义信息（而非仅依赖语法规则），SETYPE展示了语义感知方法在漏洞检测中的有效性，并能够在真实应用中发现零日漏洞。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection via static analysis traditionally relies on security experts encoding insecure coding patterns into algorithmic rules. However, this approach often focuses on syntactic patterns and overlooks deeper semantic information in the code, such as the meanings of variable and function names. As software systems grow more complex, modeling vulnerabilities using only syntactic rules becomes increasingly challenging.
  In this paper, we propose a semantics-aware approach to detecting software vulnerabilities. We present SETYPE, a semantics-aware type system that can be derived directly from source code based solely on the meanings of symbols and expressions in natural language. In the SETYPE type system, both type inference and checking are performed by Large Language Models (LLMs), and a failed type check indicates a potential vulnerability.
  We prototype PYSETYPE to demonstrate the feasibility of SETYPE for detecting vulnerabilities in Python web applications. Our evaluation on real-world applications achieves 87% detection precision and 88% detection accuracy. Using PYSETYPE, we identified 15 potential zero-day vulnerabilities, nine of which were confirmed by developers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Agentao，一个受治理的本地优先运行时系统，用于工具型LLM智能体。该系统通过分层架构将模型生成的动作提议与宿主授权的执行分离，将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹作为显式运行时抽象，使智能体更可治理、可检查和适合宿主控制的本地环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益成为执行系统，能够调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久记忆并与外部协议交互。这些能力带来了过度权限操作、弱可审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不可控副作用等安全风险，亟需一个能够有效治理智能体行为的运行时系统。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构设计，包括宿主面对表面、宿主契约、运行时核心、权限中介工具系统，以及用于记忆、重放、插件、技能、子智能体和协议集成的支撑子系统。核心思想是将模型生成的动作提议与宿主授权的执行分离，并通过权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹作为显式运行时抽象来实现治理。

Result: Agentao展示了如何通过运行时抽象实现权限管理、状态追踪、协议边界控制和执行轨迹的可检查性。系统代码已公开发布在GitHub上，但论文明确指出该系统不提供形式化的安全保证。

Conclusion: Agentao证明了通过将治理机制构建为运行时的一等抽象，而非事后附加措施，可以使LLM智能体更加可治理、可检查和适用于宿主控制的本地环境。该系统为构建安全的工具型智能体提供了架构层面的参考。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [136] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个误解的分层分析框架，将误解视为一个"产生分歧→放大分歧→检测与修复"的分层过程，从九个跨领域文献中识别出11种精确失效模式，归纳为8个分析层，并进行了形式化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着沟通从实时面对面转向AI中介渠道，沟通者失去了传统修复手段所依赖的资源，误解检测问题变得日益紧迫，而现有研究缺乏统一的分类框架。

Method: 整合九个通常互不引用的研究领域的观点，识别出11种失效模式；基于文献推导出8个分析层而非采用现成模型；对8个层进行形式化建模，将信息与传播理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建；提供证据矩阵、编码手册和9个对话案例。

Result: 发现11种失效模式分别作用于沟通过程中的特定点位：8种主要产生分歧，2种主要放大已有分歧，1种决定分歧是否被检测和修复；提供了可审计的证据矩阵和形式化模型。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于首次将误解机制定位于沟通过程的具体节点并按功能分类，超越了以往任何误解分类研究，为AI中介沟通中的误解检测提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [137] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricForge方法，通过反射式进化从小规模带真实标签的轨迹中自动生成智能体评判准则，用冻结的7B模型作为代理和评判者，在无环境奖励的条件下实现可归因的人类可读评分。相比通用G-Eval评判器，其原始一致性无显著提升，但关键优势在于将失败轨迹误判为成功的假阳性率降低约一半（0.115 vs 0.173），这在奖励无关的评估场景中更具部署意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型代理评估越来越依赖另一个语言模型作为自动评判器，因为可执行环境奖励作为黄金信号昂贵、缓慢或部署时不可用。现有评判器要么手写评分标准（如G-Eval），要么微调评判器权重，两者都倾向于给流畅但不成功的轨迹计为成功。需要一个能真正反映真实结果的奖励无关评判代理。

Method: RubricForge方法：从一小批带有真实标签的轨迹中归纳生成智能体评判准则文本，通过反射式进化（reflective evolution）迭代优化准则，使其与真实环境奖励的一致性最大化，然后冻结该准则并应用于保留轨迹。整个过程只需一次模型调用，无需环境访问。产出的准则为人类可读文本，每个判定可归因到命名标准。

Result: 在tau-bench（173条标注轨迹，来自220次回滚）和WebShop（160条）上，使用同一冻结7B模型作为代理和评判器，主要增益是忠实度而非原始一致性。与G-Eval相比，一致性差异不显著（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准上通用评判器略优（|err|差值-0.048，p=2x10^-4）。但RubricForge将失败轨迹过度计为成功的概率约减少一半（假阳性率0.115 vs 0.173），在WebShop排名上更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。
